# Third-party notices

repose is MIT. It ships one executable that links the Rust crates below and compiles in the
vendored files after them. Each crate is under the licence its authors chose, given as its
SPDX expression from the crate metadata, and every licence, copying and notice file found in
its package (or, for a crate that publishes none in its package, fetched from its repository
into licenses/, see licenses/SOURCE.md) is reproduced in the Licence texts section, each
distinct text once. The list is
the closure of normal dependencies from the repose binary, not entered through proc-macro
crates; build-only, dev-only and proc-macro crates are compile-time inputs and are not listed.
Generated by scripts/third-party-notices.ps1 from `cargo metadata --locked` for x86_64-pc-windows-msvc;
run it after a dependency change (the gate fails when this file is stale).

## Vendored files

### PowerShell grammar (crates/app/syntaxes/PowerShell)

`PowerShell.sublime-syntax` from <https://github.com/SublimeText/PowerShell>, MIT, compiled
into the executable; provenance and the one local edit are in `SOURCE.md` beside it. Its
licence:

```

```

## Not shipped

Git is a prerequisite the user installs; repose runs the git on PATH and bundles none. The
editor and terminal it opens (VS Code, Windows Terminal, pwsh) are likewise the user's own.
Segoe UI and Cascadia are read from the Windows font folder at run time, never copied; the
fallback fonts are those the egui crates above bundle, under the licences listed for them.
